“The first time…” Kara hesitates undoubtedly not wanting to relive the memory, “We didn’t know how bad it was going to be. How much we could get out of her in one session.” The blonde’s voice is shaky and tears are threatening to fall over the rims of her eyes so Lena grabs her hand and intertwines their fingers, hoping it brings the same calming sensation that it would if the roles were reversed in this instance.

“It was bad enough I had the image of her bloody, lifeless body fresh in my head.” Her eyes found Lena’s, the horror behind blue orbs unmissable, “Not to mention, you hadn’t woken up yet. We weren’t sure if you even would at that point. Ellie somehow thought it was her fault and her instinctive need to fix everything reached an entirely new level.”

“Sounds familiar.” Lena couldn’t help but say.

“Up to this point we’ve had a pretty good success rate.” Kara forced out a humorless laugh, “We both were with her. Ellie and I.” Kara clarified, “She started using her powers and at first it was fine. But I don’t know what happened, it all just escalated so fast.”

“What happened?”

“Exactly what you’d probably expect knowing what you do now.” Kara said with a sigh, “It didn’t seem like much at first. She was using her powers fine and then all of sudden it was like a switch was flicked and the next thing I know she’s on the ground screaming. Blood coming out of her nose and ears. We tried to stop it but Lennox couldn’t control anything anymore. Not her powers. Not her emotions. She just lost it.”

“It overwhelmed her.” Lena resolved.

Kara nodded, “It was like she didn’t know how to control her powers anymore. The sound from her super hearing was overwhelming and she was unintentionally crushing everything with her superstrength. And the more overwhelmed she got the more she seemed to be using her powers which in turn…”

“Released more kryptonite into her body.”

“Exactly.” Kara’s eyes fell to the floor to avoid having to look at Lena, “She nearly killed herself because she couldn’t stop once she hit a certain point.”

“Which would explain the power dampener.”

“It was the only thing we could think of to make sure nothing overwhelmed her and she didn’t accidently release to much kryptonite into her body at once.” Kara looked back to Lena and gave her a sad smile, “She’s going to be okay though. Alex has been working with her to help her control her powers, especially once the kryptonite starts distributing through her system. We’ve got it down to a science at this point, it’ll just take a while. I’m just glad we have Alex because knowing what the effects of kryptonite feel like…” The blonde paused and shook her head, “I just can’t watch Lennox be slowly tortured like that.”

Lena brought her hand up and cupped Kara’s cheek, letting her thumb rub softly against her cheek bone, “I’m sorry.” She said sincerely, “I know it’s not my fault but I made those devices and Lex is my brother and somehow that makes me feel responsible.”

“Nobody blames you.” Kara put her hand over Lena’s and turned her head just enough to let her lips press a soft kiss to the woman’s hand, “You’ve been through just as much as Lennox during this whole thing.”

“I still can’t believe you let an eighteen year old take over my company.” Lena said trying to lighten the mood a little.

Kara let out a soft chuckle. She knew L-Corp was basically Lena’s whole world back in the past. She put everything she had into it, dedicated nearly every ounce of her time and energy into turning Luthor Corp into the completely opposite of what her brother had done with it. In many ways the company was Lena’s baby, so Kara understood the reaction. But the Lena she knows now probably hasn’t even thought about L-Corp in weeks.

She had more important things in her life these days. Actual babies. An actual family to pour her time and energy into.

“I may be biased because she’s our daughter but Lennox is much more impressive than a typical eighteen year old.” Kara defended, “So is Ellie.”

Lena stopped to ponder over that, the idea easing her mind. Kara standing and moving towards the doors of her office caught her attention and when Kara gestured for her to follow she did. Kara led them down to Lena’s lab and Lena’s mouth fell open at the sight of it. It was filled with new tech that was obviously far more advanced than anything she was used to in her time.

“Pretty neat huh?”

“Neat?” Lena asked dumbfounded, as if the word didn’t do it anywhere near justice, “It’s incredible Kara.”

Kara bit her lip, adoring the way Lena looked like a kid in a candy store, her eyes wide with wonder, and her fingers clearly itching to reach out to get her hands on some of the stuff she had never seen before as she wandered the room. Some things never change.

Kara walked over and leaned against one of the countertops, crossing her arms over her chest and watching the woman she fell in love with and her mind raced with everything they’d been through together in their lives.

The battles between their families before they had even laid eyes on each other, meeting Lena in her office for the first time and saving her later that day, their journey to becoming friends, confessing her biggest secret to someone who could’ve easily been her biggest enemy in another life, dancing along the lines of friends and something far more, finally having the dam break and falling in love, and it all leading to where they are now.

She sighed happily, her eyes finding Lena’s when she looked up and she couldn’t hold the words back, “I know you’re going to go back and J’onn is going to completely wipe your mind, so you won’t remember any of this.” Kara stated hesitantly, pushing her body away from the counter and towards Lena, “But I just wanted you to know, even if it’s just for a moment that you won’t remember, that you accomplish so much.”

Lena couldn’t take her eyes off of Kara. The proud tone of her voice and the genuine smile on her face when she stopped in front of her and gently tucked a strand of dark hair behind Lena’s ear making her heart pound in her chest, “We.” Lena corrected softly, “We accomplish so much.”

Kara’s grin only grew, “We are quite the team.”

“A Luthor and a Super.” Lena shook her head lightly with a small laugh, “Who would’ve thought?” Kara beamed down at her, pressing a light kiss to her cheek, “Can I ask you something Kara?”

“Of course.”

“Why’d you want to show me Catco and bring me to L-Corp?” Lena asked curiously, “Not that I didn’t love it because seeing all this is incredible. And I know this is a temporary distraction from Lennox’s procedure more than anything else but out of everything that the future probably has to offer? Why this?”

Kara turned her head a little and stared across the room as if trying to get her thoughts in order. Lena let her. Waiting patiently for the blonde to say something.

“I always believed that it’s what brought us together.” Kara confessed a little shyly, finally looking back at Lena, “I mean it’s how we met. Me being an employee at Catco and being sent with Kal to interview you.”

“You mean question me?” Lena smirked knowingly.

“That’s not the point.” Kara returned with a sly grin of her own, “The point is that I was able to get to know you because I was a Catco journalist who kept showing up at L-Corps doors. And it’s where we fell in love.”

“Kara Danvers, you really are a sentimental sap.”

“Stooop.” Kara dragged out, a blush growing on her cheeks as she shoved lightly at Lena and dropped her head back, but the dark haired woman grabbed onto Kara’s arms and kept herself close. Kara brought her head back up to smile down at her, allowing her arm to slip around Lean’s waist loosely, “It’s Luthor-Danvers by the way.”

“That’s extremely bold of you to take the Luthor name you know?”

“The most incredible person I ever met in my life was a Luthor you know?”

“Mmm, is that so?”

Kara let out a chuckle at the flirty look on Lena’s face and she offered a nod, “Yes.” She promised, dropping a kiss to Lena’s cheek again before sighing, “I better get you back to the DEO.” Kara said reluctantly, “Lennox is probably done by now and I’m sure the twins are waiting to take you back.”

Lena nodded, stepping up to Kara and waiting to be hoisted up yet again into familiar strong arms, “Thank you for showing me all of this. It’s incredible.”

Kara smiled at the woman in her arms, “It’s the least I could do after unleashing a set of twins on you unexpectedly and then bringing you to the future to assist in saving your own life.”

Lena had to bite her tongue to keep a laugh from slipping out. Because it wasn’t funny. Lennox could’ve died and she herself could’ve died. But hearing the statement now, knowing that everyone was safe, sounded like some sort of joke. Another insane chapter of her life that will be completely erased from her mind by the end of the day. A scenario that she won’t even live through for somewhere around two decades.

She supposed though she signs up for it all somewhere along the way. Has already signed up for it back home, involving herself with an alien that throws herself at danger and trouble for the sake of others. Not that she herself was any better at staying out of trouble.

“I wish I didn’t have to forget it all.”

“I know.” Kara said with a sad smile, “But don’t worry. This life is all yours and you’ll be here living it before you know it.” The blonde let out a deep sigh, thinking about how fast time has flown by, “And then you’ll wish you could go back and live it all again like you’re about to.”

That made Lena smile because Kara was right. This was all hers and nobody could take it away from her.

She would get to love Kara for the rest of her life and have the family she always longed for. And she was just starting this journey. So when they get back to the DEO and she was walked back into the room that they were going to open the portal to take her back home in, she tried her best not to feel sad. Instead she tried to be happy for once because everything was going to be okay.

“I guess this is goodbye then.” Lena said, facing Kara.

“Don’t worry,” Kara said, a small tease to her voice, “you’re going to spend so much time with me from then to now that you’ll be sick of me by the time you get back to this time.”

“Doubtful.” Lena playfully argued.

“Alright, we get it. You’re in love.” Lennox harassed lightheartedly with a roll of her eyes, “You’re literally going to see her right away when you get back to the past. It’s hardly an occasion for a goodbye.”

Lena felt a small blush creep up her cheeks but shook it off quickly and turned to the twins, “And what about the two of you?” She asked, “I won’t see you for who knows how many years.”

“Don’t pretend like you haven’t already done the math in your head.”

Lena let out a guilty laugh, a giddy feeling deep in her chest at the awareness of how well these people actually know her. And none of it surprises her anymore. But there’s a fondness there that, even though she knows she’ll get back with time when the twins arrive somewhere down the road in her life, she’s going to miss for the time being and doesn’t entirely want to give up. But she knows she has to, so instead of dwelling on it all she just steps forward and wraps her arms tightly around Lennox who is standing closest to her.

Lennox hugs her back just as tightly, “Ellie is going to take you back.” She informed Lena who pulled back a little to look at her daughter’s face, trying to convert it to memory even though she knows it’s useless considering her mind is about to be wiped as soon as she gets back, “I have to stay here.” She says looking a little disappointed at that, “Alex thinks it’s best since I just had one of my extractions. But thank you for helping us.”

“Of course.” She smiled reassuringly and brought her hand up to cup Lennox’s cheek, “I think you and Ellie probably deserve most of the credit. I should be the one thanking you two.”

Lennox hugged her one more time before stepping back, “See you in a few years.” She said with a beaming smile that made one just as large grow on Lena’s face.

“Ready?” Ellie asked coming up beside her, device in hand.

“Yes.” She said with a sure nod, glancing behind her one last time where Kara and Lennox stood beside one another.

“You go straight there, make sure their memories are wiped, and then you come right back Ellie, okay?” Kara reminded, “No funny business this time.”

“Don’t worry, I’m leaving all the trouble with you.” The blonde sent a smirk towards Lennox who owned it with a proud look on her face and a small shrug of her own.

Lena let Ellie wrap a protective arm around her as she opened the portal, sending one last wave over her shoulder before Ellie pulled her through and she was flashing back into her reality. They landed in Lena’s penthouse, Ellie landing graciously and helping her stay steady on her feet before she let go.

“Home sweet home.” Ellie spread her arms out and gestured lazily around the room with a wide smile on her face, “Happy to be back?”

Lena smiled genuinely. She was happy to be back to her familiar surroundings. A world she knew and a future that for once in her life she was excited for.

“I should probably call Kara.”

“No need.” Ellie said with a knowing smile, a light thud sounding from the balcony outside drawing both of their attention.

Sure enough Kara was standing out there, clad in her super suit. The blonde looked timid as she made her way inside and Lena knew that she was silently asking a question when she gave her a worried look.

“We did it.” Ellie answered for her, “We saved her.”

A beaming smile suddenly appeared on Kara’s face as she looked to Lena who nodded. Next thing she knew she was wrapped up in strong arms and her feet were off the floor, “Kara!” She squealed when the blonde spun her around happily.

“I love you.” Kara whispered in her ear when she set her back down on the floor, still holding her close for another moment before she pulled away, “So,” She said with a curious gleam in her eyes, “how was the future?”

“You knew?” Lena asked in surprise.

“You didn’t think we would be irresponsible enough to just take you without letting her know, did you?”

“Well-” Lena started but she was immediately cut off by Ellie holding up her hand.

“You know what, don’t answer that. I keep forgetting Lennox is involved in all of this and if it was up to her we probably would have.”

Lena and Kara just shook their heads, neither being able to stop the smiles on their faces but Kara’s quickly faded as her gaze lingered on Ellie for a moment longer and then shifted to Lena’s face, “I talked to J’onn.” Kara admitted quietly, “He should be here any minute to…you know…mind wipe us.” She informed hesitantly.

Kara didn’t really love the idea of someone being in her head. Sorting through her thoughts and memories to take away ones that she just got used to having. And she knew Lena didn’t want to let go of them either. But they didn’t have a choice in this and they both were highly aware of that. In order for everything in their life to play out normally as it was intended to, they have to do it.

“I’ve been in these clothes all day.” Lena said off handedly, shrugging out of Kara’s light hold and taking a step towards the hallway that led to her bedroom, “I should change before he gets here.”

Kara and Ellie both nodded in permission and Lena trekked off to the back of the house, slipping out of her clothes and exchanging them for a much more comfortable outfit of a pair of jeans and a sweater. She moved into the bathroom, splashing some cold water on her face and trying to prepare herself for what was about to take place. Although she figured it wasn’t that complex. She just had to sit there and J’onn would do the rest.

She knew when he was done she wouldn’t know the difference. There would be no pain or missing anyone or impatiently longing for what the future brings.

For some reason though it still makes her sad. She looks at herself in the mirror one last time and lets out a deep sigh before moving back into the bedroom. She stops in the doorway, surprised to see Ellie standing next to her dresser. Lena cranes her neck to see what the girl is looking at and sees her digging through a jewelry box sitting there.

“J’onn is here.” Ellie says out of nowhere and it startles Lena slightly because she hadn’t realized Ellie even knew she was in the room but then she remembers the super hearing and feels a little dumb.

“Already?”

Ellie doesn’t look at her, just nods and twirls something between her fingers.

Lena is curious over what seems to have caught the girl’s eye and so she walks over to stand next to her, “It was my fathers.” Lena states when she gets close enough to see the large men’s ring that Ellie appears to be studying. And as Lena gets a good look at the large dark green emerald attached to it, she reminds herself to maybe put that one away in the safe in her closet rather than a box out in the open that anyone could take.

“My father wasn’t the best man.” Lena admits suddenly, flashes of her late father coursing through her head and she’s not sure why she’s telling Ellie this but for some reason it just starts spilling out of her, “But he was always good to me.” She admits, a small smile gracing her lips, “When he got sick, I was still fairly young and wasn’t aware of all of the questionably immoral things he was a part of. It seems to pale in comparison to the things Lex ended up doing down the line, but nevertheless corrupt actions existed amongst him.” Lena inhaled deeply through her nose, letting it out slowly to shake the emotions spiking in her, “One of the last times I saw him before he passed, he told me that he felt like his wrong doings had finally caught up to him.”

“I know.” Ellie said mindlessly before finally tearing her eyes away from the ring and looking at Lena, “I’ve heard this story before.”

That surprised Lena. She hardly ever spoke about her father. Even Kara hadn’t heard much about him. Lena had shared some of the good memories she had of her father with Kara, had pushed through revealing some of the bad, his struggle with alcoholism that Lena had once feared was a bad habit that she had taken on herself, and of course the change in her life once the man had passed and she was left in the malevolent hands of Lillian and Lex.

“Have you?” She asked, knowing she was trying to coax something out of the girl but not sure what exactly it was she was looking for.

Ellie nodded, “After…” The blonde glanced back down to the ring, her eyes drifting across the engraved words on the inside of the band before letting her fingers run across the detailed sides with a sigh, “After you woke up the first time, Lennox was still doing pretty bad. Not physically.” Ellie offered up when she saw the confusion on Lena’s face, “Mentally.” She clarified, “Uncle Lex really got in her head.”

Lena nodded slowly, knowing firsthand how easy it was for Lex to be manipulative. She had been on the receiving end of his schemes and calculated control for a lot of her life and she hated that she ever allowed her brother to get his hands on one of her children.

“He convinced her she was fated to be just another Luthor.” Lena stated knowingly.

“Lennox struggled more with control than I did when we were growing up.” Ellie explained, “Not in a bad way, she just was never afraid to use her powers. She’s always liked how they’ve made her feel.”

“Powerful.” Lena caught on.

Ellie sighed sadly and shrugged, “She hasn’t told me everything that happened while he had her in that place, but I know he turned that powerful feeling that she had into something she should feel guilty about. Tried to convince her that they were alike somehow.”

“She is nothing like him.” Lena practically growled.

“I know that.” Ellie said, reaching up to put a comforting hand on Lena’s shoulder, “And she does too. But whatever he did to her gave her doubts about who she is. At least until you woke up.” She admitted, which caught Lena’s attention, “She didn’t tell me everything you said to her but she told me the story you told her about your dad, and fate. And that you gave her this.”

Lena watched the girl’s fingers wrap around her father’s ring and toy with it in her hand.

“I gave that to Lennox?” Lena asked in surprise.

The ring had been in her possession since her dad gave it to her a couple nights before he had passed away. It was one of the few sentimental things she had left from her father and she can’t imagine herself ever giving it away to anyone. But as she looks up and see’s Ellie’s curious green eyes staring back at her she changes her mind. Because now she doesn’t have to desperately hold onto old memories of getting the slightest feeling of being loved directed at her and loving someone else. She has real people that she cares about and loves. And who love her back. And she knows without having even experienced it all that she would give anything to any of them if it made them feel better.

“She put it on a silver chain and wears it around her neck. She hasn’t taken it off since you gave it to her.” Ellie drops the ring back into the jewelry box with a soft chuckle, “You know we aren’t ashamed of being Luthors. We’re proud of it. Same as we are about being Danvers’.”

“I know.” Lena responds, but she drops her head a little anyways.

She does know now that Luthors aren’t synonymous with bad in the future, but it still is now in her present life and she’s filled with that same guilt that Lex must’ve instilled in Lennox in the short time he spent with her.

Being a Luthor did make you feel powerful. But in all the wrong ways. And she knows how Lennox must’ve felt with Lex whispering in her ear about all the power she could have if she sided with him, it was a tactic he had used on her many times before. Promising her world domination, standing side by side. And she’s felt the same guilt she imagined Lennox did for sometimes enjoying feeling so powerful. Like just having the option alone made her bad.

“I’m worried about her.”

Ellie’s words pull Lena out of her thoughts, “Why’s that?” And it feels like a stupid question considering everything they’ve all been through in the future but she asks anyways.

“She pretends that being trapped in that place with him didn’t mess her up even though it’s obvious that she still has trauma from it all. I mean she can barely sleep through the night. She wakes up screaming a lot like that.” Ellie said, referring to when Lena witnessed one of Lennox’s nightmares back in her office when she first arrived, “It takes her a second to realize she’s not there anymore. She’s scared she’s going to hurt someone.”

“He made her think she’s dangerous.”

“She is.” Ellie argues, “We all are.” She said referring to Kara, Lennox, and herself.

And the way she says it, she knows that in some way Lex’s effects on Lennox have somehow seeped over to Ellie too. But Lena won’t have any of that.

“No.” She states matter-of-factly, “You’re not.” Lena brings her hand up to gently cup Ellies face and gives her a soft smile, “I know we haven’t known each other for very long,” She jokes light heartedly, proud of herself for the small lift that appears on the corner of the blonde’s mouth, “but I see so much of your mother in you.” Lena confesses, “And I have known your mother for a long time and she is dangerous in theory, sure. She has a lot of abilities and if she wanted to she could tear this entire city to the ground. But she would never. And do you know how good you have to be to have all that power and strength and not even dream of using it for bad? You have powers that could be dangerous but because of who you are, you are not dangerous. None of you. I don’t care what anyone else thinks or says.”

“Look at Lex.” Lena continues, “He had none of those things. No powers. No otherworldly abilities. Nothing. Just a human being. And he’s far more dangerous and has done more damage to this world than any of the three of you ever will. And it’s not because he’s a Luthor, it’s because he’s a narcissistic xenophobic asshole who craves power and is willing to do anything to get it. People like him are the real danger to society, not you or your sister or your mother.”

Ellie seems to mull it over and eventually nods, “I guess I never thought of it that way.”

“Don’t doubt yourself.” Lena practically demands, surprising herself when she pulls the girl into a hug herself and drops a kiss to the side of her head. But Ellie seems to appreciate it, sinking into Lena without hesitation.

After a moment they pull away from one another, “We should probably…” Ellie lets the words trail off and gestures to the door.

Lena nods, almost having forgot that J’onn and Kara were waiting on them, “Right.” She says a little tentatively but she offers a smile anyways, “Of course.”

As soon as Lena turns her back Ellie rushes to pull the envelope that had been tucked into the waistband of her jeans from her hip and slips it into the large jewelry box before shutting it gently. She lets out a sigh, questioning herself for a second before she follows Lena out into the living room where J’onn and Kara are waiting.

“Ready?” Kara asks when she sees the pair and when Lena nods, Kara’s blue eyes go to Ellie, “I know we’ll see each other again someday in the future but I still feel like I should say goodbye.”

Ellie smiles and doesn’t hesitate to wrap her arms around Kara, “Bye.” She whispers, “For now.”

Kara smiles but it falters when she meets Lena’s sad eyes staring back at her. She lets herself hold the girl for another second before she pulls away and looks to J’onn, “Let’s get this over with.”

J’onn nods and sits Lena and Kara down on the couch, Ellie standing off to the side shooting instructions at J’onn as a reminder of what to do despite him already have gone through this with Alex just before he had come to Lena’s. It doesn’t take long before every memory of Lennox, Ellie, and the rest of the events that had taken place involving them are being pulled from their heads. And it feels like it’s all over as soon as it starts. J’onn’s eyes popping open and taking an uncertain step back waiting along with Ellie to make sure it worked.

“What are you doing here J’onn?” Kara asks, standing up and facing the man. She looks confused because they never bring any DEO business to Lena’s penthouse.

J’onn clears his throat not sure what to say, “Umm sorry, we came to see if Miss Luthor had found anything that could help in those samples we gave her from the scene from downtown last week.”

“I already gave the data regarding the samples to Agent Danvers.” Lena informs politely, her eyes flickering to Ellie standing awkwardly off to the side, the action drawing Kara’s eyes to the girl as well.

“Who is that?” Kara asked.

“Oh, I’m an intern.” Ellie states quickly with a bright smile, “Getting a feel for being an agent out in the field.”

“We have interns at the DEO?” Kara asks J’onn with furrowed eyebrows.

J’onn nods, quickly waving her off, “It’s a new program.” He states, already moving towards the door, “Sorry to intrude for no reason, I wasn’t aware that you had already talked with Alex. We can show ourselves out.”

“Yes. Of course.” Ellie said, throwing one last wave over her shoulder and following J’onn out of the suite.

Kara and Lena both waited in silence until they heard the door close, “That was weird right?” Kara asked, looking over at Lena who nodded.

“I’m so exhausted though that I am just going to ignore it.” She said, already reaching for Kara, “Can we just go to bed?”

Kara let out a soft giggle, “Aww come on Lena, it’s movie night. And we haven’t even eaten yet.”

Lena rolled her eyes and made her way to the couch, “Fine.” She said, reaching for her phone and tossing it towards Kara without looking, knowing that Kara would catch it even if her throw was way off, “You order Chinese and put a movie on. But no promises that I stay awake through the whole thing.”

“Long day at work?” Kara asks, already dialing the number of the Chinese place that they both love from the other side of town and Kara knows she can fly over there and have the food back a lot faster than it’ll take someone to deliver it but she likes doing things the normal way with Lena sometimes so she doesn’t mind.

“Yeah.” Lena responds but it comes out unsure because as exhausted as Lena feels she can’t even remember what she did today, “What about you?” She asks once she hears Kara order their food and get off the phone, the blonde already making her way over to her, “Any big attacks on National City today?”

Kara stops shy of the couch, looking up and thinking it over, “No. No, I don’t think so.”

“You didn’t take a hit to the head did you?” Lena teases when she hears the doubt in the blondes tone. Kara huffs and goes to join the woman on the couch but Lena puts a hand on her chest and tugs on the cape still hanging from Kara’s shoulders, “Out of this first.”

Kara smirks, “Oh so it’s going to be one of those kinds of movie nights.” That earns her a fairly hard smack to the chest and a shove from Lena which does nothing but cause her to let out a laugh, “Okay, okay.” She flashes to the back of the house and is back in front of Lena before the dark haired woman can blink and she can’t help but smile brightly at the slightly annoyed look on Lena’s face, her dark hair blown messily off to the side from the wind she created, “Sorry.” Kara apologizes sheepishly, reaching out to push it out of her face for her.

“No you’re not.” Lena smiles anyways and lets Kara, who is now in a pair of shorts and an L-Corp sweatshirt that she gave the blonde, crawl onto the couch and cuddle up next to her.

And for a second it makes her nervous because the action doesn’t make her tense up like it did when they first started getting close. And Kara doesn’t hesitate before wrapping her arms around her waist, just does it and turns her head to the large tv that is already playing the Wizard of Oz. It’s starting to feel normal and comforting and neither of them has dared to ask what this is that they’re actually doing now.

But Lena thinks she’s okay with it for now because she doesn’t know how long it’ll all last before she inevitably does something to screw it up. So instead of worrying about it she sinks her body further into the couch and brings a hand up to run her fingers softly through Kara’s hair.

She can already feel her eyes getting heavy and she doesn’t know when she drifts off but she knows she couldn’t have lasted long because she’s woken up by Kara climbing off of her and announcing that the food is here. So she rubs her eyes and sits up, let’s Kara eat most of the food she had ordered for them, and when it’s all gone and the movie is over she lets Kara carry her to bed.

She falls asleep with the blonde’s body wrapped around hers but when she comes to in the morning Kara isn’t in bed with her. She sits up and rubs at her eyes, stretching out before tossing the covers off of her and swinging her feet to the ground. She can smell coffee coming from the kitchen and makes her way out there expecting to find Kara but the blonde is nowhere to be found. The balcony doors are slid open though and it’s an easy giveaway that Kara got called out to tend to some kind of Supergirl duties.

So she pours herself a cup of coffee and carries it back to the room with her. Taking her time to enjoy a hot shower and when she steps out and wraps a towel around her she remembers promising Kara that she’d take this Sunday off and spend the day with her. So she doesn’t bother to put makeup on and instead of going to her walk in closet to put on her work clothes, she moves to the dresser in the bedroom that’s filled with her more casual wear.

She makes her way across the room and over to her dresser and pulls out a pair of jeans, slipping them on, followed by one of her more comfortable bras and a long-sleeved shirt to offset the cool air of the morning coming in from the open balcony doors out in the living room.

She closes the top drawer and is about to turn to head out of the room when the edge of something sticking out of her jewelry box that’s sitting on top of the dresser catches her eye. She lifts the top to find an envelope with her name written across it and an anxious feeling settles in her stomach. She looks over her shoulder and surveys the room. She doesn’t see anything out of the ordinary but Kara is the only other person who has been in her home anytime recently and it puts her on edge because she knows the handwriting on the envelope doesn’t belong to the blonde.

She grabs it anyway and slowly tears it open. She slides out the note slipped inside and unfolds it, letting her eyes scan over the words and it just makes her eyebrows furrow deeper. She reaches back inside the envelope and pulls out the small flash drive inside and twirls it around in her fingers, studying it for a second before glancing back at the note.

“Morning.”

Lena startles, instinctively shoving the envelope and its contents out of sight, her free hand going up to her chest and turning her head to see Kara coming into the room with her hands up and an amused grin on her face, “Sorry,” The blonde offers, “I didn’t mean to startle you.”

Lena shakes her head, taking in the super suit donning the blonde, “It’s fine. I just didn’t hear you.” She drops the envelope and note onto the dresser when Kara comes closer to her and slips an arm around her waist, “Supergirl duties already?”

“Criminals apparently don’t take Sunday mornings off.”

Lena leans into Kara when she drops a kiss to the side of her head, “And unfortunately neither can I.” Lena decides, the note lingering in her mind and she can’t help it, she has to get to her lab immediately and see what’s on the drive.

Kara’s arm falls and she takes a small step back to send Lena a look of disappointment, “I thought you had the day off.”

“I just got a call from the office and I have to go in.” Lena lied, already seeing the protest in Kara’s face, “I know, you just lectured me on needing to work less-”

“Because it’s true.” Kara interrupts.

“It is.” Lena agreed for once, hoping it’ll get Kara to not press further, “But this is important. I promise to not be too long.” She swears, “I just have to do one thing and then we can spend the rest of the day together like we planned.”

Kara purses her lips, thinking it over, “Can I come with you?”

“No.” Lena said a little too fast and a little too harshly, surprising Kara. She would usually let Kara tag along but she didn’t know what she had just received and she didn’t want to drag the blonde into something before she even knew what it was. Lena cleared her throat, “I mean, it’s that project I’ve been working on. You know, the classified one?”

“I’m a part of the most classified organization in National City.”

“The DEO isn’t as big of a secret as you’d like to think it is.” Lena taunted lightly, not being able to hold back the laugh that fell from her lips at the offended look on Kara’s face. She pressed her lips against Kara’s, blindly reaching back for the envelope and note and grabbing it before pulling away, “Go get yourself some breakfast, change out of your suit, relax here for a little bit, and I’ll be back before lunch, okay?”

Kara groans but agrees, planting one last kiss against Lena’s lips before pulling back, “What’s that?” Kara asks with a sly grin, grabbing Lena’s wrist gently without even having to look away from the woman’s eyes.

Lena flustered, “Oh just some notes.” She knows Kara’s not buying it but the blonde just shakes her head.

“Please do not get yourself into any trouble.” Kara lets go of Lena, looking at her with pleading eyes, “We’ve almost made it through an entire month without an attempt on your life, let’s not ruin it on a perfectly good Sunday.”

“Don’t jinx it.” Lena teased on her way out of the bedroom door, Kara not being as amused as she is by the light hearted joke.

“No criminal activity Lena.” Kara calls out, “I mean it!”

Lena turns to find Kara trailing behind her, now out of her suit and in the lounge wear she had on last night and the woman’s super speed never seizes to impress her, “Now where would the fun in that be? I am a-”

“I can’t handle a Luthor gene shining through comment this morning.” Kara rolls her eyes at the grin that slides onto Lena’s face as she walks out the front door.

“See you at lunch.” Lena says, locking the door behind her and heading straight to the elevator. She doesn’t bother to wait for her driver, heading down to the garage and driving herself to L-Corp. She rushes to her lab and pulls the envelope out of her purse, dropping the bag to the ground carelessly and pulling out the flash drive to slip it into her computer.

She waits anxiously for something to pop up on the computer screen, not knowing what to expect. When a file shows up she opens it and her jaw drops when pages of blueprints start to open on her screen. She clicks through them, her eyes scanning in awe. But that eerie feeling in her stomach never leaves because she’s staring back at her own completed design to something that she hasn’t finished yet.

The blueprints are much more hashed out and advanced than what she has as her prototype now. And there are additions that she didn’t come up with herself. And she’s not sure what’s going on or how to react to it. So when she’s done scrolling through everything and feels like she has a systematic understanding of it all she grabs the note that was in the envelope with the drive and studies it again for a moment.

‘You will understand someday. As hard as it may be, just trust me on this one.
Love from someone outrunning fate, E.A.L.D’

The words at the end once again made a deep furrow form between her eyes. Lex is the only other person she knows who would know about them. And that had that unnerving feeling resurfacing. But the letters at the end meant nothing to her. No matter how hard she tried or how many hours she spent to come up with an acronym, an organization, initials that could belong to someone, anything; she came up short.

She had made it home by lunch that day. But the blueprints and note and what she should do with it all kept her up at night for weeks. Her anxiety was high and she felt like she was on high alert all of the time, waiting for her brother to pop up and have something to do with it, but that never happened.

So she went about building her prototype the way she had originally planned, ignoring the blueprints in entirety. And then she finished, perfecting her device to her satisfaction. And it prompted her to pull up the blueprints for the first time in nearly a year. Still contemplating whether or not she trusted them and whether or not to make the additions to her now completed device.

….

Ellie popped out of the portal to find Kara and Alex waiting for her. They gave her a second to gather herself as the portal disappeared behind her and then Kara was in front of her, hand on her shoulder.

“Well?”

“Everything went fine.” Ellie said with a proud smile, “Memories were wiped, every trace of Lennox and I completely gone, and everything is completely back to normal.” She said, waiting for someone to confirm that everything was still in fact normal, “Right?”

“So far nothing seems out of the ordinary.” Kara approves, “You did good El.”

Ellie smiled brightly again but then she caught Alex’s eyes and she could see the question in them before the woman even asked, “You went straight there and back?” Ellie nodded, “You didn’t do anything but get Lena back and make sure J’onn was able to clear their heads?”

Again Ellie nodded, “Didn’t even leave her building.”

Alex raised her eyebrow and Ellie squirmed under the intensity behind her aunt’s stare, “Your mother wants to see you.”

Kara looked over her shoulder at Alex and then back to Ellie in confusion, “Am I missing something here?”

Ellie shook her head, “Nope.” She dismissed, shrugging Kara’s hand off of her shoulder and making her way towards the door to go find Lena, “She probably just wants to make sure I made it back okay.” She said casually, waving any worry off, “You know how mom is.”

She was out the door before Kara or Alex could say anything. Once in the hallway she realized she hadn’t asked where Lena was, stopping to tune into her super hearing and find Lena. L-Corp.

She knew she wasn’t supposed to be flying around in her street clothes but she decided that this one time her parents could make an exception so she launched herself out of the DEO and straight to the balcony of her mother’s office where she could see the woman sitting at her desk, not even bothering to turn around when she landed a little heavily on the cement outside. She sighed and flung the door open, stepping into the office warily.

“I see the antidote is working.” She said, stepping in front of the desk and facing her mother who looked back to her normal self.

Lena’s eyes didn’t flicker up at all to look at Ellie, “Yes.” She said simply, like she was in a meeting with a client and Ellie let out another sigh, dropping into the chair in front of Lena’s desk, “Alex believes that the metahuman abilities will slowly ware off over the next week or so and my vital signs are all healthy.” She added, “I should make a full recovery.” Ellie smiled at that. The stress from the last handful of weeks falling off her shoulders but the smile slowly faded when Lena finally looked up at her with a disapproving look.

“Ellie Alura Luthor-Danvers.”

“Yeah?” Ellie answered, knowing what was coming well before Lena slapped the note she had written down and slid it across the desk so it was sitting in front of her.

“I spent years trying to figure out what those letters stood for.”

“You always have liked a challenge.” She tried to deflect.

“Ellie.” Lena shot back sternly, clearly not finding it comical at all, “What were you thinking?”

“I was thinking that I could help Lennox.” Ellie argued, standing up out of her chair, “You’ve seen her during those extraction procedures, we’re torturing her. Why wouldn’t I stop that if I could? It was a small addition to a device that already existed.” She explained her reasoning, “I figured it couldn’t cause any harm.”

“We don’t know that for sure.” Lena argued back, trying to keep her composure. She sighed leaning back in her chair, “You know how fragile the timeline is. Any little change can potentially have catastrophic consequences.”

“Aunt Alex and mama already said that everything seemed to be intact and normal.” Ellie crossed her arms over her chest and stood her ground.

Lena sighs when her daughters eyes drop to the ground, “It was a big risk.” She said, standing up and walking around her desk to stand in front of Ellie, reaching out for her.

“Lennox would have done it for me.” Ellie pauses for a second before she brings her eyes up to meet Lena, “You would have done it for either one of us.”

And they both stop, neither saying anything because they both know that it’s true.

“Did it at least work?” Ellie asked, not being able to take the silence anymore.

Lena stared at her for a moment before walking back around to the other side of her desk and pulling one of the drawers open. She pulled out a plastic bag from her lab and tossed it onto the desk in between them.

“Are those?”

“Alex took them out herself about an hour ago.” Lena supplied and now Ellie knows why her Aunt had questioned if she had done anything she wasn’t supposed to do in the future, “Alex was pretty suspicious when I suddenly remembered I had included a…” Lena paused, looking at Ellie, “What exactly would you refer to it as?”

“An invisible kill switch.” Ellie answered with a delighted smile, “Shuts down the entire device, locking up every mechanism, and stopping distribution from the device instantaneously and indefinitely no matter what’s programmed to be dispersed from it.” She said with a shrug, “You know, just in case there’s ever an emergency.”

“An emergency? Like someone reprogramming it to poison someone with Kryptonite?” Lena teases.

“Yeah. Something like that.”

“It was actually quite brilliant.” Lena admitted, not being able to hide how impressed she truly was, “Did Lennox know about it?”

Ellie shook her head, “I thought it would be best to keep this one to myself for the time being. Why?”

“Outrunning fate.” Lena repeated the words from the note, a shaky breath slipping from her lips, “Lennox must have told you about my father. Why did you include it in the note?”

“It’s the only thing I could think of that would get you to trust the note without actually knowing who it was from.” Ellie explained, “Without actually giving you any information about the future.”

Lena nodded, thinking it over for a second, “Well whatever she told you about what I told her, it clearly did not get my father’s message across.” Lena rolled her eyes at the mischievous glint in Ellie’s eyes, “She’s finally starting to rub off on you and I’m not sure I like it.” Lena teases, “My father used to always say that you can’t outrun fate. It’s one of the last things he ever said to me.” She admitted a little sadly, “It always catches up to you.”

“I don’t think Lennox believes that.” Ellie smirked when Lena let out a small huff and rubbed at her temple, “You know how she likes to put her own spin on things.”

“I do.” Lena states with a nod and a fondness in her voice. She after all was the one who is probably the most responsible for teaching Lennox to challenge and question things.

“Speak of the devil.” Ellie said out of nowhere and Lena furrowed her eyes but when her office doors burst open and Lennox came striding in with a beaming smile almost to wide for her face, Lena shook her head.

“You sneaky bitch.”

“Lennox!” Lena reprimanded but the dark haired girl ignored her and beelined straight for Ellie.

“I can’t believe you left me out of your scheming.” Lennox barreled into Ellie, wrapping her arms around her sister’s neck causing them to stumble back a few steps together. Ellie let out a loud laugh, squeezing her sister tightly, “Look at you breaking rules and getting away with it.” Lennox pulled back and cupped Ellie’s cheeks with her hands, “Leaving a cryptic note in the past. That’s so badass El.”

Ellie let out another stream of giggles, swatting Lennox’s hands away from her. When she finally squirmed out of her sister’s hold she took a step back and scanned Lennox looking for any signs of distress, “You’re okay then?”

Lennox smiled brightly at her, doing her best to keep her emotions at bay when she felt her eyes starting to water slightly, “Better than I’ve ever been thanks to you.” Her eyes drifted over to Lena who was smiling at the scene, “And mom’s back to normal too. As you can see she wasted no time kicking me out of her office and getting back to work.”

“Well the two of you keep me on my toes.” Lena walks over to the twins and slings her arm around Lennox’s shoulders, “I leave you two and your mother unattended for a couple of weeks and all hell breaks loose.” Lena reaches her free hand out and grabs the silver chain hanging from Lennox’s neck, tugging the ring that’s dangling from the end of it out of the girl’s shirt and holds it in between her fingers, “I think you misconstrued the message I was trying to give you when I gave you this and told you that story about my father.”

A furrow settles between Lennox’s eyes, “How so?”

Lena’s eyes linger on the words engraved inside the ring, “You can’t outrun fate.” She reads out loud, sighing as she drops her arm from Lennox and lets the ring at the end of the chain fall back down to where it hangs low on her chest, “That was his moto. He was an alcoholic and was a greedy businessman who did a lot of bad and cruel things in this world. He believed that he was fated to end up that way. Withering away in a hospital bed in a lonely mansion far bigger than anyone needs. His actions caught up to him. There was no way to get away from them.”

“Same with Lex. No matter what you get away with for the time being, you can’t outrun the inevitable.” Lena swears, believing it whole heartedly, “I told you about that because you aren’t one of them. And I wanted you to believe that you had a different fate.”

“And I do.” Lennox said, “But you also told me that when you were still sick.” She reminds her mother, “And I know you told me that story because part of you believed that you were fated to end up like them. And now that I met that version of you that you used to be, I know that you at least once believed that you were going to end up just like them. Alone.”

Lena dropped her eyes to the ground, not having expected that and she was about to object but Lennox continued, “You may have told me that to make me believe that I was destined for something different. That Ellie and I had a chance to be something better for the Luthor bloodline but you already were that. But you still don’t always believe it. And I know when you were withering away in that hospital bed,” She echoes the word of Lena explaining her father’s fate, “that you were trying to get me to accept that there was a good chance you were going to die. You were trying to leave me with something because you thought that you couldn’t outrun fate and that that was yours catching up to you.”

“But that’s not true.” Ellie jumped in this time, “If that’s your fate then we didn’t care what we had to do. We don’t care what your dad told you. Or uncle Lex. Or anybody.”

“I know you think you can’t outrun fate but,” Lennox shot a glance over to Ellie before a smile crossed her face and Lena knew she was going to say something stupid already, “we’re like really fast.”

Lena couldn’t help the genuine laugh that came out of her at that one and she nodded, “Yeah that’s definitely not what I was trying to get across originally.” She admits but for some reason she feels proud that Lennox somehow came to the conclusion she did.

“So fast we outran fate twice.” Ellie adds, raising her hand up high which is immediately met with Lennox’s slapping against it in a high five.

“Hell yeah!” Lennox shouted, “We ran circles around fate. Made it our bi-”

“Okay, okay. Enough.” Lena cut Lennox off and started grabbing her stuff and made her way towards the doors of her office, “I get it. You two are abnormally fast.” She said, smiling at Lennox who grabbed her bag off her arm and carried it for her, Ellie sliding her hand into the crook of Lena’s arm and leaning into her as they walked, “ No more outrunning fate though, okay?”

“Only in absolute emergencies.” Lennox agreed, Ellie nodding along.

“It was actually all pretty exhausting.”

“Oh yeah?” Lena asked as they stepped into the elevator, “You didn’t enjoy your trip to the past?”

“No, that was pretty fun if I’m being honest.” Lennox said.

Ellie nodded in agreement, “Stressful but definitely pretty cool.”

“Well I hope you enjoyed it because you’re never going back.”

Lennox and Ellie both laughed, “Not planning on it.”

Lena was thankful for that. Happy that the day was over. Happy that this entire situation that her deranged brother put them in was finally over. She felt at peace for the first time in months and all she wanted to do was crawl into bed.

And that’s exactly what she did once she got home. She got home, showered, changed into her favorite pajamas, grabbed a book off the shelf, climbed into bed, and waited for Kara to come home. Which didn’t take long.

“She did something didn’t she?” Kara asked as soon as she walked through the doors of their bedroom. Lena gave her one look and Kara knew the answer. She dropped down onto her back on the bed dramatically and threw her arm over her eyes, letting out a groan, “Ellie’s supposed to be the good one.”

Lena laughed at that, setting her book down on the nightstand and reaching over to tug on Kara’s arm so she could see the woman’s face, “She is.”

Kara cracked her eyes open and looked at Lena, “What’d she do?”

“Nothing that you have to worry about.” Lena promised, not wanting to stress Kara out more than she already was. She pulled lightly on Kara’s arm, nodding her head towards the top of the bed to get her to crawl up.

Kara got the message and moved to the head of the bed, leaning her back against the headboard and letting Lena curl up beside her. Lena was under the covers, Kara still sitting on top of them still clad in her super suit, but neither of them minded. Kara crossed her legs at her ankles and dropped her head back against the headboard, “What she did wouldn’t have anything to do with you suddenly remembering that your devices design included a way to completely stop it from functioning so we could safely remove the two devices in Lennox, would it?”

Lena hummed, “It may.”

Kara let out another groan, “Do you know how many times and how in detail I went in explaining the importance of not messing with the past?” And the grouchy look on the blonde’s face forced Lena to bite her lip to prevent a smile from gracing her lips, “Who knows what Ellie could’ve messed up by doing that.”

“You would’ve done it if you thought of it.”

Kara looked down at Lena offended, “I certainly would not have.” Lena gave Kara a disbelieving look and Kara’s strong stance on her hypothetical actions faltered, “Fine maybe I would have but that is not the point.”

“What is the point?”

“I don’t know.” Kara huffed in defeat, “Aren’t we supposed to be like disappointed that they didn’t listen to us. Or give them some kind of punishment or something?”

Lena let out another laugh, “You want us to be disappointed and dish out punishments for what exactly? Saving my life and saving Lennox from having to endure who knows how many of those treatment sessions with Alex.” Lena pulled her head off of Kara’s shoulder and looked up at her with a raised eyebrow, “And if I remember correctly, you’re the one who sent them back into the past in the first place. So maybe you should be the one receiving a punishment.”

Kara got a mischievous grin that Lena recognized immediately but she couldn’t stop her in time, “A real punishment or a fun punishment?”

“Eww!!” Lennox’s voice broke through the house followed by Ellie’s, “Disgusting!!”

“You knew they were listening didn’t you?” Lena quietly asked although she already knew the answer.

“Of course I did.” Kara whispered unnecessarily, dropping a kiss to Lena’s lips before pulling away, “Why are the two of you spying on us?” Kara asked just barely louder than a talking voice and Lena glanced over to the door, waiting for the twins to appear.

Ellie popped her head in first, a sheepish look on her face, “Sorry. We didn’t mean to. We were just coming to get you guys and we didn’t want to interrupt.”

“It’s late.” Lena said, not really minding though, “What did you two need?”

“Nothing.” Ellie admitted, “Just thought we could spend some family time together.” She said with a shrug.

“We brought pizza.” Lennox exclaimed, finally popping into the room.

Kara didn’t hesitate to straighten up and hop off of the bed, “Well why didn’t you say so.” The blonde said, leaning across the bed and reaching for Lena who let her grab her arm and help pull her up and off the bed.

Kara turned ready to drag Lena along with her but Lena stood her ground and Kara was yanked back. She turned to face Lena with a frown, looking down at their joined hands and giving an experimental tug, Lena again not budging. Lena smirked and let Kara pull on her harder than usual but the super strength was no good because now Lena had it too. At least for now. So why not have fun with it.

“No super suits at the dinner table darling.” Lena said gently, kissing Kara on the cheek and dropping her hand before moving past her and the twins.

Kara frowned, the twins laughing behind her, “Don’t worry they’ll ware off.” Ellie promised before going to follow Lena.

“And good thing too.” Lennox added, “She’s already the most powerful one in the house without her metahuman abilities, could you imagine if she had that to hold over us as well.” Kara laughed when Lennox shook her head, huffing out an, “Insufferable.” before turning and heading towards the kitchen.

Kara let out one more laugh, smiling to herself and quickly changing into her normal clothes. She made her way through the house and down the hallway, stopping in the entryway and taking a moment to take her family in.

Lena was hovering over the stack of pizza boxes that was currently on their kitchen counter, trying to fend off Lennox who already had three slices stacked on her plate. Ellie was pulling drinks out of the fridge with a smile on her face, closing the fridge eventually and setting them out on the counter. Lennox reached across to grab a soda and moved to the table, Lena putting a couple of slices on a plate and handing it to Ellie so she could join her sister. Lena finally looked up and caught Kara’s eyes, sending her a soft smile.

“Come on.” Lena said, grabbing another plate and carefully placing a few slices on it, “Your kids have already practically blown through an entire box.”

Kara finally walked out into the room, sending a playful frown towards Lena, “My kids?”

“Well they certainly didn’t get their appetites from me.” She said, offering the plate to Kara who took it and joined their kids at the table, Lena following behind her with a modest plate of one small slice of pizza and a glass of water which made Kara laugh.

“I have a feeling you’re going to need more than that until those metahuman abilities ware off.” Kara said and she couldn’t help but smile as they fell into their normal comfortable conversations as a family.

It was all easy. Like two of them hadn’t just nearly died. Like they hadn’t had to go back in time to prevent just that from happening. But Kara was thankful for that.

Thankful that her family was here and they were all going to be okay. So she enjoyed her night. Laughing along when Lennox and Ellie recounted their time in the past and the stories that came with it. Allowing the memories from that time in her life to creep back in and envelop her. And when the night was over and the twins headed off to bed, as exhausted as she and Lena both were, she was more than happy to lazily tangle her limbs with her wife and remind her of how much she loved her even though she was aware that Lena already knew.

When they finally settled, the night had already disappeared and the sun began to creep up and light up National City. And Kara had never felt more content in her life than in that moment, curled around her wife like they were one, their kids safe, and her world finally back in order the way it should be.
